### Part 124 — `databank/missions/chapter-05-sun-set-to-die.md`
**Context:** Chapter 5 — For the Sun is Set to Die (v3.4) > Mission 1 — Hero, Honor That Crimson Call > Meet Up with Dan Heng
**Kind:** personal
**Id:** `chapter-05-sun-set-to-die__meet-up-with-dan-heng`

*(The Flame Reaver suddenly froze, as if something unforeseen had transpired.)*

**Zeph the Guard:** (Trailblazer), please stay close to us. We need to rendezvous with Lord Dan Heng.

**Mnemosyne the Guard:** We will do everything in our power to clear the path for you!

**Zeph the Guard:** There he is! Lord Dan Heng!

**Mnemosyne the Guard:** Quick! We must assist him!

**Dan Heng:** ...(Trailblazer)!? What are you doing here? Where's Phainon?

> *(Trailblazer)* It's a long story. Let's deal with that guy first!
> *(Trailblazer)* We need to go, Dan Heng!

**Mnemosyne the Guard:** We are under Lord Phainon's orders to escort both of you to safety, no matter the cost! Leave this to me and the Holy City Guards... Zeph, take them and go!

**Dan Heng:** At some point earlier, they suddenly froze in place... not moving at all. Like... some kind of omen.

> *(Trailblazer)* Now's our chance to take them down!

**Dan Heng:** No, don't get too close! That thing's black flames are burning hot. I don't know what's happening, but leaving is the best option.

---

### Part 125 — `databank/missions/chapter-05-sun-set-to-die.md`
**Context:** Chapter 5 — For the Sun is Set to Die (v3.4) > Mission 1 — Hero, Honor That Crimson Call > Screwllum's Revelation
**Kind:** personal
**Id:** `chapter-05-sun-set-to-die__screwllum-s-revelation`

**Dan Heng:** Phainon... is he going to be alright?

> *(Trailblazer)* Leaving like this... I'll definitely regret it.

**Dan Heng:** I... feel the same. Someone should stay and help Phainon complete Era Nova. For now, let's get back in contact with the Express and plan our next move. That's the safest course of action. We need more information.

**Screwllum (appearing):** On that note, I have a proposal— I hope to convey a message to the two "Nameless." It has been a while, Nameless of the Astral Express. I'm glad I arrived in time to stop that black-clad one from harming you further.

**Screwllum:** I fully understand your situation. For now, the only thing I can do is temporarily freeze that black-clad one in place. Time is limited. So, I'll keep this brief— The place where you stand, Amphoreus, is a world constructed from data and memoria, an experimental field of a certain Aeon. And now, this isolated sandbox world is rapidly falling into a third Path — Destruction.

> *(Trailblazer)* A Lord Ravager... Like Phantylia?

**Screwllum:** The disaster that this Lord Ravager intends to trigger may be far more severe than the Luofu's Stellaron Crisis. Because its goal is... The Aeon of Erudition, Nous.

**Screwllum:** Putting aside the complex details of the inferences involved, I have a request to make, for one of you Nameless, to stay behind on Amphoreus, acting as an informant, in order to prevent the wrath of Destruction from ravaging the cosmos. This world is enveloped in a "firewall" that defies explanation, and the flow of time here is also different from reality. Madam Herta and I can only tear open a gap and project images inside.

> *(Trailblazer)* I will shoulder this responsibility.

**Dan Heng:** Mr. Screwllum, I believe you, so I only have one thing I'd like to confirm with you. As Amphoreus is already facing its end, how can we guarantee the safety of the person who stays behind?

**Screwllum:** Look at this, if you would— This Curio is called a "Chronocognitive Anchor." The two of you may view it as a kind of Space Anchor in the world of Erudition. As long as you carry it with you, your individual data structure will be protected and will remain unaffected by the influence of digital tsunamis.

*(The connection becomes garbled.)*

**Screwllum:** Two ███ final ███ messages. One: The Intellitron ██████ named ███████ Lygus ████ possibly █████ the primary culprit behind everything. Two: Herta ██ discovered a clue ██— Miss March █████ has ████ already been ████ drawn into ███ Amphoreus██

*(Trailblazer obtains Chronocognitive Anchor. Dan Heng departs for the Express.)*

---

### Part 126 — `databank/missions/chapter-05-sun-set-to-die.md`
**Context:** Chapter 5 — For the Sun is Set to Die (v3.4) > Mission 2 — (Side) Mother, Parted by the Turning of Seasons > Meet with Your Friends

**March 7th:** Now it's... Dan Heng and (Trailblazer)?

*(Approach Dan Heng and the Trailblazer)*

**March 7th:** Hey guys... what are you doing in a place like this?

**Dan Heng:** March! There you are...

**(Trailblazer):** ...finally.

**Dan Heng:** The Trailblazing journey just isn't the same without you.

**(Trailblazer):** ...

**(Trailblazer):** Traveling's no fun without you around.

**March 7th:** Well duh! What's Team Trailblaze without its star member?

**March 7th:** Wait a second... You might look like (Trailblazer) and Dan Heng, but...

**Dan Heng:** We understand your confusion. The Garden's allies helped us establish this neural link to reach you.

**March 7th:** The Garden's allies... you mean Miss Black Swan?

**Dan Heng:** Yes, she helped make this possible.

**(Trailblazer):** Keep going, March.

**(Trailblazer):** Find your past, wake up... and travel with us again.

**March 7th:** ...

**March 7th:** That sounds... really tempting. Finding my past and continuing to trailblaze new worlds with you...

**Dan Heng:** Trust in yourself, March. It's gotten us this far, hasn't it?

**Dan Heng:** Follow us. Let's go through that gate together.

### Part 128 — `databank/missions/chapter-06-before-their-deaths.md`
**Context:** Chapter 6 — Before Their Deaths (v3.5) > Sub-Mission 1: Time, Ferry Me Through Ages > Explore the Mysterious Space
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-06-before-their-deaths__explore-the-mysterious-space`

### Part 129 — `databank/missions/chapter-06-before-their-deaths.md`
**Context:** Chapter 6 — Before Their Deaths (v3.5) > Sub-Mission 7: Captives, Behold the Expanse Beyond Light > Glimpse the Beginning of the Next Story
**Kind:** personal
**Id:** `chapter-06-before-their-deaths__glimpse-the-beginning-of-the-next-story`

**"Zandar":** I have to admit, the twelve factors' emulation of life in this experiment has far exceeded my expectations.
**Herta:** Make your choice then, Senior Society Member. Do you want to keep walking down the wrong path and be remembered for your incorrect conclusions...
**Herta:** Or do you want to return to the audience's seat and give yourself, Amphoreus, and the entire cosmos a more fitting end?
**"Zandar":** I must refuse. A dead man never fears death.
**Herta:** Did I not make myself clear? Have you forgotten about our dear "Lord of Silence"? You wouldn't want her to consider your eight other clones as cancers within the Erudition and chop them all up, would you?
**"Zandar":** ...
**"Zandar":** Your logical deductions are vastly different from mine. Therefore, allow me to provide one more academic recommendation.
**"Zandar":** Listen, my peers. Why don't you look beyond this world, just as the Chrysos Heirs did? Re-evaluate your calculations, and take into account all enemies in the upcoming war among the Aeons.
**"Zandar":** Amphoreus is not a place where three Paths "entangle," but where three Paths "fight to the death."
**"Zandar":** You used memoria to resist me, but did you not consider the possibility that...
**"Zandar":** **The Remembrance and THEIR children might have taken this opportunity to join the fray as well?**

*(In the meantime...)*
**???: ** ...
**???: ** Ohh. So this isn't just silence. It's the answer.
**???: ** "What was the last thing (Trailblazer) said to you before you parted the waters at the Scalegorge Waterscape?" ...A tricky question, but one with a simple answer.
**???: ** He didn't say anything back then, right?
**???: ** That's correct. And now, I'm sure...
**Dan Heng:** You are not March 7th. There's no way she would remember that detail.
**???: ** ...
**Dan Heng:** I'll only warn you once. Leave her body. Now.
**???: ** How impulsive. That's not like you at all.
**???: ** Why don't you calm down and think about another possibility? I didn't take over "March 7th's" body...
**???: ** Rather, it's March 7th who regained "my" memories.

*(Unlock Achievement "Before Their Deaths")*

### Part 134 — `databank/missions/chapter-07-back-to-earth.md`
**Context:** Chapter 7 — Back to Earth in Evernight (v3.6) > Mission 2 — Homecoming, Within Sight Yet Beyond Grasp > Head to March's Room and Clear Out the Intruder
**Kind:** personal
**Id:** `chapter-07-back-to-earth__head-to-march-s-room-and-clear-out-the-intruder`

*(After the battle)*

**Dan Heng:** Is the trap now taken care of?

**Himeko:** ...Dan Heng!

**Dan Heng:** Miss Himeko, I'm back.

**Dan Heng:** Even if it's just through a "spirit warp jump"...

**Himeko:** You could hear our conversations?

**Dan Heng:** Yes. It was thanks to your voices that I could pierce through the memoria barrier. Although...

**Dan Heng:** It was not a wise decision to let a Memokeeper of unknown origins board the Express.

**Black Swan:** Can't argue with that.

**Himeko:** Miss Black Swan has been on the Crew's side ever since things started going wrong in Amphoreus. We can trust her.

**Dan Heng:** So many baffling things kept happening one after another, I don't even know where to begin. I wish I could've returned with better news...

**Himeko:** Don't blame yourself. You're all safe and sound, and that's good enough for me.

**Dan Heng:** Are (Trailblazer) and March 7th really safe and sound?

**Black Swan:** As a matter of fact, while you were on your way back, Amphoreus's circumstances changed once again.

**Black Swan:** And these circumstances have something to do with the two you just mentioned.

*(Black Swan told Dan Heng what happened to (Trailblazer)...)*

**Dan Heng:** Are you saying that (Trailblazer) has been kidnapped by an unidentified Remembrance pathstrider?

**Himeko:** So you're saying that person's appearance is identical to March 7th?

**Black Swan:** It's too early to say that as I haven't seen her with my own eyes. I've only detected a similar aura in the Memory Zone.

**Black Swan:** We have no way of knowing what March 7th has experienced. But I can assure you, if we sink too far into the memoria tide of that "Miss March"...

**Dan Heng:** What will happen?

**Black Swan:** Everyone here will be torn to pieces.

**Black Swan:** Please trust me on this.

**Dan Heng:** ...

**Himeko:** ...But I also trust in our Trailblazing companions. I know this isn't what March would want.

**Dan Heng:** No matter what secrets are buried within, we'll do whatever we can to find that "March 7th," and bring back (Trailblazer) and the March we all know.

**Dan Heng:** I mostly understand what we've learned about the Erudition and the Destruction, but what roles do the Remembrance's intruders play in Amphoreus?

**Black Swan:** I made some new discoveries related to that with Madam Herta's help.

**Black Swan:** As part of the deal, do you mind if I share them with you?

**Black Swan:** Alright then... Fourth Mirror?

**Fourth Mirror:** It's finally my turn to shine? Ooh, this feels like such a grand occasion!

---

**Fourth Mirror:** It's really been too long since I last appeared!

**Black Swan:** This... friend here is Madam Herta's very capable assistant. They were the one who helped you complete the "spirit warp jump."

**Black Swan:** Additionally, it was thanks to them that I was able to learn the secrets of that group of extremist Memokeepers.

**Fourth Mirror:** I appreciate the praise, but I didn't have to lift a finger — I mean, mirror!

**Dan Heng:** You did this through a mirror?

**Black Swan:** The Garden has strict rules for collecting memories, but not all Memokeepers are as... proper as I am.

**Black Swan:** Herta Space Station often has uninvited guests, and they never seem to know their limits. That group of Memosnatchers has been taken care of, and they are now trapped in the Fourth Mirror.

**Black Swan:** How many of them are there? I can't quite remember.

**Fourth Mirror:** ...I didn't really count carefully either. Maybe around forty-two? There's still room for more!

**Black Swan:** I carefully dissected the memories of these "colleagues" of mine. Individually, their goals are not worth mentioning, but when you piece them all together...

**Black Swan:** ...You come to a conclusion that can't be ignored.

**Black Swan:** The Garden of Recollection is secretly salvaging the memories of a Path disappearing.

**Black Swan:** In other words... their goal is to gain the memories of a God's death.

**Himeko:** The moment an Aeon falls? That would indeed be a spectacular sight for someone dedicated to recording these things.

**Black Swan:** That's exactly the problem. They seem to have been aware of the Destruction's movements early on, but they lacked a decisive power and had no way to penetrate Amphoreus's firewall.

**Dan Heng:** So what did they decide to do?

**Black Swan:** Hide the truth and wait for an opportunity. Now it seems that letting Memokeepers guide the right people here was also part of the plan.

**Black Swan:** This also confirms one of my suspicions: They have already infiltrated deep within the Garden.

**Dan Heng:** They would not hesitate to associate with the Destruction in order to salvage memories... Could they be The Cremators?

**Black Swan:** I don't know. Perhaps there are even deeper things at play here...

**Black Swan:** The majority of Memokeepers believe that when the universe inevitably reaches its death, unable to sustain increasing entropy, Fuli will use the "memories" of everything to reforge the cosmos.

**Black Swan:** Amphoreus, as a Scepter, can be viewed as a mini "universe." This is why Memosnatchers have been frequenting the Herta Space Station.

**Black Swan:** Without a doubt, these extremists wish to record the cause and effects of Irontomb's destruction of the Erudition. They may want to use it to simulate the end of the universe, or to probe into the unfathomable will of the Aeons.

**Black Swan:** The Trailblaze broke the seal around Amphoreus, giving these people the opportunity to enter. Now, they'll do anything to prevent outside powers from interfering.

**Black Swan:** Because your involvement will certainly influence Irontomb's final outcome.

---

**Dan Heng:** You said that March is closely linked to the Remembrance. Is what she's doing related to these Memosnatchers?

**Black Swan:** I do not believe that "Miss March" is on their side.

**Black Swan:** In fact, when the Car you used for landing first made contact with Amphoreus, a violent surge of memories flowed past. Combined with what we know now, I have reason to suspect that the flash of power came from...

**Dan Heng:** March.

**Black Swan:** Exactly. She was the first to be hijacked by the Remembrance, but she used memoria to neutralize the firewall, allowing you two to enter Amphoreus.

**Dan Heng:** ...

**Himeko:** At present, we've lost contact with (Trailblazer). Our only choice now is to rely on the eyes and ears of those two geniuses to understand the situation within Amphoreus.

**Himeko:** There's no use in agonizing over this, Dan Heng. Our top priority now is to find where that Car landed.

**Dan Heng:** ...Yes.

**Pom-Pom:** Passenger Dan Heng! Y—You finally found your way back home!

**Dan Heng:** Yes, conductor, I'm back. It's just...

**Pom-Pom:** Pom-Pom knows! Pom-Pom came over to bring you good news!

**Pom-Pom:** Passenger Welt says they've found those two passengers!

---

**Himeko:** Welt, and Mr. Screwllum.

**Welt:** Himeko, you seem to be feeling much better now.

**Welt:** And Dan Heng, it's great to be able to talk to you. Once again, we owe Madam Herta a huge favor.

**Screwllum:** Madam Herta doesn't care much for favors, but she'll be happy to receive everyone's praise.

**Screwllum:** Back to the topic at hand... Mr. Dan Heng, we did a sweep of the area surrounding Amphoreus's celestial body for organic signals, and we finally discovered the bodies of you and (Trailblazer).

**Screwllum:** The two of you were very fortunate as there was something protecting your bodies. All vital signs are still present, and your functions are all intact.

**Dan Heng:** Was it... March protecting us?

**Screwllum:** Our preliminary determination is that the "bubble" enveloping you two in the vacuum is made of a substance similar to memoria. Conjecture: This substance is connected to the lost Miss March.

**Welt:** It will take some effort to bring your bodies back to the Express, so you should get some rest first.

**Dan Heng:** ...

**Dan Heng:** Have we completely lost contact with the inside of Amphoreus?

**Screwllum:** There have been several unforeseen circumstances. We broke through Lygus's firewall after the "Law" was amended...

**Screwllum:** But now Amphoreus is enveloped by the fierce Path of Remembrance, once again cutting off all channels of communication for outsiders.

**Dan Heng:** So we have no way of contacting (Trailblazer).

**Dan Heng:** If we take into account the difference in the flow of time, every minute we delay here could be a year of waiting for (Trailblazer)...

**Himeko:** Dan Heng... What are you thinking?

**Dan Heng:** If it was March who helped us get past the firewall initially...

**Dan Heng:** Then does that mean only (Trailblazer) and I have the "travel permit" for Amphoreus right now?

**Screwllum:** That is a logical inference, Mr. Dan Heng.

**Dan Heng:** ...Ah.

**Dan Heng:** Mr. Screwllum, do you have a way to transmit my spirit signal back to Amphoreus?

**Welt:** You want to go back? That is... far too risky.

**Dan Heng:** Even if my body and spirit are able to return without a hitch, there's not much I can do here.

**Himeko:** We can't lose you again, Dan Heng.

**Dan Heng:** Miss Himeko, if we can't get (Trailblazer) and March back...

**Dan Heng:** How is that any different from losing me?

**Himeko:** You're right. I should've known you'd say something like that.

**Welt:** Hah... Well, I suppose there's no point in trying to dissuade you.

**Dan Heng:** Mr. Yang, Miss Himeko, I will do everything in my power to bring the Express Crew back together—

**Dan Heng:** No one will be left behind.

*(Switching to Trailblazer's POV... When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.)*

*(Now Amphoreus is ensnared by the Remembrance, one of the Three Paths, and March and (Trailblazer)... they are nowhere to be found. I'm the only one who can bring them back. Amphoreus is shrouded in mystery now, but I have a friend who can help me see through the fog.)*

**Dan Heng:** How long can the encryption key you wrote for me last, Mr. Screwllum?

**Screwllum:** "Please rest assured that I will extract you on time before the encryption key expires."

**Dan Heng:** But that means I will no longer be able to hack in via the same method.

**Dan Heng:** I only have one chance.

**Screwllum:** "The maze of the Remembrance is starting to change. I cannot remain your guide on your path forward."

**Screwllum:** "That is why we had to form a temporary alliance—"

**Screwllum:** "With our former enemy."

**"Zandar":** I express my utmost respect for your courage to personally face such dangers.

**Dan Heng:** Lygus...

**"Zandar":** Your enmity is well within my calculations.

**Screwllum:** "Allow me to reiterate that Lord Anaxagoras now has a firm grasp on your crucial weakness."

**Screwllum:** "If you still plot to harm the Nameless, then you will find that the Society's previous warnings were not empty threats."

**"Zandar":** ...I understand. I'll only do what needs to be done.

**Dan Heng:** ...

**Dan Heng:** I never imagined that I'd one day walk alongside a genius who offered himself to the Destruction.

**"Zandar":** My stance has never changed. The Erudition's ruin is inevitable.

**"Zandar":** But before that... I'd be very willing to witness the reunion of the Nameless and to escort you on your way home.

**Dan Heng:** More like her appearance completely interrupted your plans, even forcing you to seek out "collaboration."

**Dan Heng:** I'll remember that.

**"Zandar":** Your words are as sharp as ever, but remember, "happenstance" is the only true constant in the universe. The demise of the Progenitor of All Dragons is a testament to that.

**"Zandar":** Come with me. I am honored to guide a Scion of Permanence.

---

**Dan Heng:** You created this wasteland before us with your own hands.

**"Zandar":** Such sights are no longer able to stir anything in my heart.

**Dan Heng:** Are you saying that, once upon a time, you were actually moved by their defiance?

**"Zandar":** I'm sorry to say, I never was.

---

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Dan Heng:** These memosprites... Is it her...?

**"Zandar":** The memetic entities of the Remembrance are everywhere. That lady wove a giant web in my blind spot.

**"Zandar":** She hacked and infected the Time Titan, converting the built-up data of Amphoreus into puppets of the Memory Zone.

**Dan Heng:** Sounds like you two have quite the history already.

**"Zandar":** Any variables that desire to influence the experiment deserve my attention.

**Dan Heng:** But you never mentioned her before. Could even a genius of the Erudition get his brains hacked?

**"Zandar":** ...Lord Dan Heng, I'm simply stating a fact. That lady is also a threat to you, a threat that you cannot afford to ignore.

**"Zandar":** She snuffed out the Remembrance without any effort and left nothing behind. Her actions were so determined... as if she had an unquenchable hatred against that Path.

**"Zandar":** Contradictions are often the keys to the truth. Lady "March 7th's" past... is a bottomless darkness.

**Dan Heng:** And? Do you expect me to join forces with you because of that?

**Dan Heng:** All I need to know is that "she" has something to do with March 7th's past.

**"Zandar":** Fine. I am only giving you a possibility for you to ponder. The choice is still in your hands.

**"Zandar":** A heavy past is like an everlasting night. As to what kinds of sins lurk within, you should know that better than I do, former Vidyadhara high elder.

**Dan Heng:** Yes, I indeed know how to "face the past" better than you do, First Genius.

**"Zandar":** I have no intention of refuting that. Let us keep going, Lord Dan Heng.

---

---

### Part 141 — `databank/missions/chapter-07-back-to-earth.md`
**Context:** Chapter 7 — Back to Earth in Evernight (v3.6) > Mission 5 — Blazing Sun, Illuminate the Path for the Lost > Defeat the Ichor Memosprite
**Kind:** personal
**Id:** `chapter-07-back-to-earth__defeat-the-ichor-memosprite`

*(Approach the next marked location)*

**"Zandar":** ...Hmm?

**"Zandar":** I didn't think there were still some that got away.

**Dan Heng:** Explain.

**"Zandar":** They are thieves from the Garden. They have long coveted this world, but had always been kept out.

**"Zandar":** They finally got a chance thanks to your "efforts." Unfortunately, they ran into "Miss March" and their consciousnesses were plucked out one by one, reducing them to mere husks.

**"Zandar":** Those who were lucky enough to escape her drowned in the black tide, becoming these twisted figures.

**Dan Heng:** But they look very different from the usual Black Tide Creatures.

**"Zandar":** The reason has to do with our destination.

**"Zandar":** Let us deal with the threats before us first. Then, I'll reveal the answer to you.

**Dan Heng:** You really have no intention of fighting, do you?

**"Zandar":** Hehe. I have no grounds to assist you.

**"Zandar":** Besides, the geniuses are still keeping a close eye on me. I would hate for anything I do to be misconstrued as provocation against you.

**Dan Heng:** ...Never mind. Let me clear the way.

*(Enter battle against Ichor Memosprite: Winged Serpent*1; Ichor Memosprite: Canine*1; Ichor Memosprite: Pheasant*1)*

---

*(After the battle)*

**"Zandar":** What splendid spearmanship. Truly remarkable.

**Dan Heng:** Let's get back on our previous topic.

**"Zandar":** These Memosnatchers attempted to infiltrate the breeding ground of the black tide to steal Irontomb's memories.

**Dan Heng:** That sounds like a surefire way of destroying themselves.

**"Zandar":** Well, our views differ on that. They had a chance to reach the truth precisely because they did not fear death.

**"Zandar":** That futile worldbearer turned himself into a seal in this cycle, attempting to suppress the black tide's spread.

**"Zandar":** His golden blood soaked into the ocean tides and covered the earth, etching the scar of Destruction into this world.

**"Zandar":** And that scar... shall point the way forward for you.

### Part 142 — `databank/missions/chapter-07-back-to-earth.md`
**Context:** Chapter 7 — Back to Earth in Evernight (v3.6) > Mission 5 — Blazing Sun, Illuminate the Path for the Lost > Head to Dawncloud
**Kind:** personal
**Id:** `chapter-07-back-to-earth__head-to-dawncloud-2`

**"Zandar":** Erudition, Destruction, Remembrance...

**"Zandar":** When the Three Paths cross once again, Amphoreus shall face a most magnificent end.

---

*(Approach the next marked location)*

**Dan Heng:** Dawncloud...

**"Zandar":** I have witnessed thirty million "futile" ends atop this cliff.

**"Zandar":** You may not agree with me, but I've always believed that this number has made me the person who understands Khaslana the most.

**"Zandar":** This is also where he decapitated me on that day and pointed his sword at the Aeon of Destruction.

**"Zandar":** He suffered a tragic defeat, but also achieved a legendary deed...

**"Zandar":** ...A drop of burning golden blood, the blood that will cleanse the world, dripped from the god's wound.

**Dan Heng:** Nanook...? Phainon managed to hurt THEM?

**"Zandar":** Yes. It melded into Khaslana's body, becoming the Destruction's final blessing.

**"Zandar":** If you wish to dispel the fog and find that "March 7th," then go and awaken his fury.

**"Zandar":** She hides in the crevices of Time in the most concealed corner of Amphoreus. Only the most ferocious will can break through the Memory Zone and illuminate her footsteps...

**"Zandar":** Just like how the blazing sun can only rise at the end of the evernight.

**Dan Heng:** How do I know this won't be another trick of yours?

**"Zandar":** I have nothing to gain from helping you two work together.

**"Zandar":** But in this cycle, Khaslana never answered my calls.

**"Zandar":** Perhaps his mind has already eroded away... Or perhaps he is still awake, still fighting the whispers of the black tide.

**"Zandar":** Deep down, I only wish that you and the esteemed (Trailblazer) could bring me an answer.

### Part 143 — `databank/missions/chapter-07-back-to-earth.md`
**Context:** Chapter 7 — Back to Earth in Evernight (v3.6) > Mission 5 — Blazing Sun, Illuminate the Path for the Lost > Touch the World-Cleansing Golden Blood and Summon Your Friend
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-07-back-to-earth__touch-the-world-cleansing-golden-blood-and-summon-your-friend`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-07-back-to-earth.md`
**Witness:** spoke

*(Sing, O Aeon! Sing of the rage of Khaslana, son of Amphoreus— A drop of burning, cleansing golden blood, melting into your sun-like blazing form. Before that drop of golden blood fell, hatred had long since consumed you. That lengthy journey made our time together feel but a fleeting moment. After that drop of golden blood fell, you've remained silent for far too long. Does your mind still hold its tenacity? Does your defiance still persist? Answer me, for I need a friend to clear the fog and unveil the sun— If your "guidance" is destined to bring rage that destroys heaven and earth, so be it, as I need a direction forward. Answer me, for only the Dawn's furor can pierce through the evernight.)*

*(Investigate the golden blood)*

**Dan Heng:** Is there really no other way...?

**Dan Heng:** How long has it been for you?

**Dan Heng:** Khaslana...

**Dan Heng:** ...Phainon, I'm back.

**Dan Heng:** What...?

**Hyacine:** "The moment you took the world upon your shoulders without hesitation... You lost the chance to ever be yourself..."

**Anaxa:** "Duty does not drive you to wield your blade, but hate. Behind that hatred, you seem to... desire the destruction of the self."

**Aglaea:** "Losses are a constant on the Flame-Chase journey. What you stand to lose weighs far heavier than life itself."

**Mydei:** "It is because you suffer that you are so much stronger than any ordinary person."

**Khaslana:** ...

**Khaslana:** (Trailblazer)... and... Dan Heng.

**Dan Heng:** You... still remember us.

**Khaslana:** Those two names... are like brands.

**Dan Heng:** Then... I'll tell you that those two names only appeared for a blink of an eye in your long journey.

**Dan Heng:** But we once fought together to protect this holy city beneath our feet... and we witnessed each other's resolve at this very cliff.

**Khaslana:** ...

**Khaslana:** I have forgotten everything except the belief I had when I set off. Even this body is as alien to me as your words.

**Khaslana:** All I know is that... I must imprison the Destruction that swallows all.

**Dan Heng:** What if I tell you that we're here for the same thing?

**Dan Heng:** Look at what this world has become. You know what it's waiting for.

**Dan Heng:** Even if you've forgotten everything, I still believe that you'll make the right choice, like you did in the past.

**Khaslana:** ...

**Khaslana:** When have we ever had a choice? When were we ever masters of this world's fate?

**Khaslana:** Even though I shoulder the dreams of millions of lives, I could only... forge the Destruction's evil will.

---

**Dan Heng:** ...No.

**Khaslana:** Everyone I know is dead, and the world I dared to save has been torn to pieces.

**Khaslana:** So... begone. This body never existed to carry out "Deliverance."

**???: ** ...Hah, there's no need to be so serious.

**???: ** Sorry. I had a really long nap, and all the bad memories woke up first.

**Phainon:** Long time no see, partner.

**Dan Heng:** Well, it was only a very brief farewell for me.

**Phainon:** We once walked side by side and trusted each other to watch our backs. That memory will never fade away, even if all else does.

**Phainon:** And because it was so precious and hidden so deep in my heart, it needed more time...

**Phainon:** To wake up.

---

**Phainon:** Trailblazing... What an interesting concept. Surely, on your worlds, that is also a Titan revered by the masses?

**Phainon:** There's no need to further confirm your resolve. Today, we inherit the duty of the gods, safeguarding the inhabitants of Amphoreus—

**Phainon:** Stand with me, and become heroes!

**Phainon:** Did you hear that? "Heroes"... I have never forgotten the weight of that word.

**Khaslana:** Even though I am no longer a hero and the only thing remaining in me is the boundless flames of wrath.

**Phainon:** But lighting a fire can also illuminate the path ahead.

**Khaslana:** Everyone I know is dead, and the world I dared to save has been torn to pieces.

**Phainon:** But there is still a group of "heroes" that I know...

**Phainon:** And they will bring the true tomorrow to Amphoreus.

**Khaslana:** ...

**Khaslana:** This body never existed to carry out "Deliverance."

**Phainon:** It only ever burned for "Worldbearing."

**Phainon:** So, I'm going to head towards tomorrow first.

**Phainon:** Don't make me wait too long, Dan Heng... (Trailblazer).

**Dan Heng:** Deal.

**Phainon:** What if we were born from the Destruction?

**Phainon:** There is no need to question your heart, no need to crave victory. If I am born to be a vessel of sin...

**Phainon:** Then I shall roar at evil, and Trailblaze a dawn for future generations to come!

---

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Phainon:** Let us go...

**Phainon:** To the other side of the night.

**Phainon:** You know that the dawn is there...

**Phainon:** And the blazing sun destined to rise.

*(Cutscene ends)*

---

**Dan Heng:** ...

**Dan Heng:** It won't be too long before our paths cross again.

**"Zandar":** There is a ruckus coming from the Grove of Epiphany. It is time for you to set out, my lord.

**"Zandar":** I have another notice for you: In this cycle, an eccentric demigod deviated from their Flame-Chase destiny.

**"Zandar":** In a history known by none, Terravox, the "Earth," fell. This matter has remained extremely mysterious, contradicting the extrapolations of thirty million cycles...

**"Zandar":** I have reasons to suspect it also has something to do with Lady "March 7th."

**Dan Heng:** As of now, clues that yield no conclusions are not important.

**Dan Heng:** Phainon already pointed me in the right direction. Open the way.

*(Black Screen: Zandar sent Dan Heng into the Exomyth...)*

---

**"Zandar":** ...

### Part 145 — `databank/missions/chapter-07-back-to-earth.md`
**Context:** Chapter 7 — Back to Earth in Evernight (v3.6) > Mission 6 — Earth, Bear the Suffering of All > Talk with the Mysterious Mountain Dweller
**Kind:** personal
**Id:** `chapter-07-back-to-earth__talk-with-the-mysterious-mountain-dweller`

*(Approach the Mountain Dweller)*

**???: ** That fiery blaze engulfed the Grove and woke all creatures from their slumber...

**???: ** And you, visitor from beyond the sky... You have no intention of listening to the cries of the "Earth."

**Dan Heng:** You are...?

**???: ** Are you surprised? Demigods with golden blood are not solely limited to Kephale's children. Is it not natural that the offspring of Georios would take on their duty?

**Terravox:** I am Terravox, avatar of the earth, divine protector of the myriad living beings.

**Dan Heng:** What a coincidence. I was just told by someone to watch out for you.

**Dan Heng:** Answer me: Why would the Dromas King of the legends, the demigod who should have fallen, appear in a humanoid form?

**Terravox:** You already know the answer: Is the Transmutation Arcanum not precisely your specialty, descendant of the Permanence?

**Dan Heng:** How do you know about...

**Dan Heng:** Was it "March 7th"?

**Terravox:** Exactly. Thanks to her, I was able to escape into the memoria tide and disappear from the world's memories.

**Dan Heng:** So you didn't die. Your "fall" was just a lie. You cast aside your duty as a Flame-Chaser and joined her. Why?

**Terravox:** To survive. For a dromas, life is no more than "existence"...

**Terravox:** The birds and beasts of this world have struggled to survive since time immemorial. Yet, unlike humans, they are unable to live on in the Worldbearer's memories.

**Terravox:** But Evernight is the master of Remembrance, and I saw your story in the Veil of Evernight—

**Ichor Memosprite:** █ ██ █Vidyadhara █ ██ the Permanence █ dragon transmutation ██ █ █resurrec █tion█ ████ █

**Dan Heng:** The path you chose to survive will lead to your death, demigod.

**Terravox:** And what of it? This body is soaked in filthy golden blood...

**Terravox:** The "Earth" is hungry, and only the Permanence can satisfy it!

*(Enter battle — Wave 1: Ichor Memosprite: Pheasant*2; Ichor Memosprite: Canine*2)*

**Dan Heng:** This madness seems exactly like a mara-struck.

*(Wave 2: Cloud Knights Patroller*3)*

**Dan Heng:** Cloud Knights? How is that possible...?

*(Wave 3: Disciples of Sanctus Medicus: Shape Shifter*2; Cloud Knights Patroller*2)*

**Dan Heng:** It's endless... Has the memoria tide returned?

**???: ** The fires of abomination reignite...

**???: ** Why not join that Water-Mirrored Moon and be fully imbibed?

---

*(After the battle)*

**Dan Heng:** Is this some... disordered memory?

**Dan Heng:** ...Ugh!

**???: ** The mists of the past shall drift away.

**Dan Heng:** What...

**???: ** Scatter, fade, turn to foam.

**???: ** Do you not remember me?

**???: ** After all, I am the heavy burden you must bear...

**???: ** How could I be so easily forsaken?

**Dan Heng:** Dan... Feng?

**Dan Heng:** Is this another Memosnatcher trick?

**"Dan Feng":** Vidyadhara are supposed to forget the past during hatching rebirth, but the Preceptors interfered back then and allowed "me" to remain deep in your consciousness.

**"Dan Feng":** If you do not wish to use that bloodstained name, you may call me "Obliviated Memory."

**Dan Heng:** ...That's not necessary. Though we've long parted ways, I won't ever forget you.

**"Dan Feng":** So, who is that giant?

**Dan Heng:** That is Amphoreus's "Earth" demigod. He should be on our side, but he was led astray and intends to seize the power of dragon transmutation.

**"Dan Feng":** The dragon descends upon the earth, and all beings look to it in reverence. You and I know that all life in the universe covets Permanence.

**Dan Heng:** Are you trying to say I'll end up like you?

**"Dan Feng":** One day you will, and as I see it, that day won't be far from the end of this journey.

**Dan Heng:** Let's move on to another topic. I have no intention of passing judgment on "Dan Feng," and I have even less desire to become entangled with him again.

**Dan Heng:** I only have one concern right now: bringing my companions back safely.

**Dan Heng:** You can either help me deal with these abominations or return to the shadows of memory, Dan Feng.

**"Dan Feng":** Alright. The filthy remnants of the memoria tide still run rampant. Let me accompany you through these ruins. You could use the help.

**"Dan Feng":** And presumably, that giant who craves the power of dragons won't just let things rest.

**Dan Heng:** Do as you please. I'm moving ahead.

---

### Part 148 — `databank/missions/chapter-07-back-to-earth.md`
**Context:** Chapter 7 — Back to Earth in Evernight (v3.6) > Mission 6 — Earth, Bear the Suffering of All > Venture into the Grove, Review the Disordered Memory
**Kind:** personal
**Id:** `chapter-07-back-to-earth__venture-into-the-grove-review-the-disordered-memory`

*(The chaotic memories here keep growing. Besides mine... Dan Feng's memories are here too?)*

*(While descending the walkway)*

**???: ** This is an interesting-looking wine jar. Was it Baiheng who brought it?

**???: ** It's a specialty from Thalassa that she brought and left here just for this gathering.

**???: ** But she herself didn't come.

---

*(Approach the memories)*

**"Dan Feng":** ...Your mind retains far more of the past than I had imagined.

**Dan Heng:** I've seen this farewell more than once in my dreams.

**"Dan Feng":** The previous life is just an immersia to a newly-born Vidyadhara. You know rationally that these are "your" memories, but it doesn't feel like you experienced them yourself.

**"Dan Feng":** I used dragon transmutation for selfish reasons, and close friends turned to enemies.

**"Dan Feng":** You probably want to ask me if it was worth it.

**Jing Yuan:** The Military Affairs Office is placing more importance on the Yuque's battle this time, and the Yaoqing called her back for a patrol mission.

**Yingxing:** Some people are born with adventure in their blood and can't stop for even a moment. No matter, I'll drink her portion for her!

**Jing Yuan:** We're already toasting? Why don't we wait for Dan Feng?

**Yingxing:** As always, the high elder has endless meetings with the Preceptors that he can't get away from. Let's not wait on him.

**Yingxing:** Pah... Isn't this just water!?

**Jingliu:** I'll relay her exact words: It is a mistake to drink too much before a battle, so I'll leave a jar of spring water from Thalassa's crystal palace.

**Jingliu:** She said that wine only has flavor after a triumphant return.

**Yingxing:** ...Why didn't you say so earlier?

**Jingliu:** I didn't want to go along with her, but she insisted on seeing your expression after your first sip.

**Jing Yuan:** Any gift from a friend, whether it is water or wine, is rich in flavor all the same.

**"Dan Feng":** ...

**"Dan Feng":** I see you still speak as if every sentence must add something of profound value.

**Dan Feng:** I see you still speak as if every sentence must add something of profound value.

**Jing Yuan:** You're just in time! The punishment for making us all wait should be three cups of wine.

**Dan Feng:** Spare me, please. I've already suffered enough next to the ancient sea.

**Dan Feng:** I've convinced the Preceptors: Vidyadhara cloudhymn magi will join the battle on the frontlines, and our armies will fight side-by-side.

**Yingxing:** It seems like you can now carry out your plan for dealing with the "Ketu Mirage," Jing Yuan.

**Jing Yuan:** Thank you for standing your ground against the dissenters, Dan Feng.

**Yingxing:** I never thought those Preceptors would ever relent. After all of this, countless Vidyadhara...

**Dan Feng:** Countless Vidyadhara will lose their lives, with no chance at a molting rebirth. We all understand this.

**Dan Feng:** But the Vidyadhara cannot become a part of the Alliance's destiny if we do not share in its joys and sorrows. We would only be bystanders to everyone else's suffering.

**Jingliu:** That is something we all understand as well.

**Jingliu:** That is also why we've gathered here. Come, raise your cups. This toast is not for bidding farewell to each other...

**Jingliu:** But for honoring the soldiers who will not return, for honoring our companions.

---

**"Dan Feng":** ...

**Jing Yuan:** Apologies, my reckless plan must have put a lot of pressure on you.

**"Dan Feng":** Nonsense.

**"Dan Feng":** If our positions were flipped, you'd support me no matter how reckless my plan was, right?

**Jing Yuan:** Of course. But let's try not to be too reckless.

**Jingliu:** When you make a decision like this, people will always ask, "Was it worth it?"... And you will also forever question yourself.

**Jingliu:** Do you know how you will answer that question?

**"Dan Feng":** I won't answer it. I will prove it.

**Jingliu:** That's right. My sword is my answer.

**Yingxing:** Thank you, Dan Feng! Thank you, Jing Yuan! Thank you, Jingliu! And thank you, Baiheng, wherever you've run off to!

**"Dan Feng":** Ridiculous, how did you get drunk from spring water?

**Yingxing:** Hmph, I'm just full of joy! It feels like I've just forged a divine weapon with masterful craftsmanship!

**Yingxing:** "I'd rather leave this world in a blaze than live until the end of time..." That's what I always firmly believed in the past. But I never truly felt like I was alive until I met all of you.

**Yingxing:** Now I wish more than anything that I could just live a bit longer. No, pretend I didn't say that!

**"Dan Feng":** ...

**Dan Heng:** It's over.

**"Dan Feng":** And you know exactly what happened to these five afterward.

**"Dan Feng":** So, can you understand it now, after both (Trailblazer) and March 7th have left you?

**"Dan Feng":** Losing a dear friend with whom you've shared so much can never be considered a victory. It's a failure that torments you for the rest of your life. I could never accept that kind of fate.

**Dan Heng:** So you made a decision.

**"Dan Feng":** I was the one who used the Transmutation Arcanum. I was the one who swore to bring Baiheng back to life.

**"Dan Feng":** And the one who brought everything past the point of no return... that was also me.

**Dan Heng:** Do you have any regrets, Dan Feng?

**"Dan Feng":** ...

**"Dan Feng":** Even if I could reverse time, I would not choose differently.

**"Dan Feng":** And if it had been Jingliu, Jing Yuan... or even Yingxing who died in battle, I'd still choose to do the same thing.

**"Dan Feng":** Because only I could make that choice and protect all of this. Now, for the sake of your companions, for the sake of those two who walk alongside you...

**"Dan Feng":** How much are you willing to sacrifice, Dan Heng?

**Dan Heng:** ...

**Dan Heng:** Everything.

**"Dan Feng":** Your answer does not surprise me.

**Dan Heng:** They are more than just my companions. They are the path I took that led me to where I am now.

**Dan Heng:** So they will also be beacons for when I am lost, leading me to the future, until my next "reincarnation."

**"Dan Feng":** Then let me see how you'll carry out your convictions.

---

### Part 150 — `databank/missions/chapter-07-back-to-earth.md`
**Context:** Chapter 7 — Back to Earth in Evernight (v3.6) > Mission 6 — Earth, Bear the Suffering of All > Talk with Terravox, the Earth Demigod
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-07-back-to-earth__talk-with-terravox-the-earth-demigod`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-07-back-to-earth.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**"Hysilens":** Be prepared for the worst, Lord Dan Heng. This Mountain Dweller must not be underestimated...

**"Hysilens":** Geocles the Mountainbreaker... Earth's Dromas Rider, it's been a while.

**Dan Heng:** "Mountainbreaker"? He's... not Terravox?

**Geocles the Mountainbreaker:** I remember you, little fish, and your despicable master.

**"Hysilens":** I didn't think you were still alive.

**Geocles the Mountainbreaker:** Death is just a return to the Earth for Mountain Dwellers. Thanks to Evernight's gift...

**Geocles the Mountainbreaker:** I was able to come back to the mortal realm and keep watch over my dear friend, the "Earth" demigod who was sacrificed to the Flame-Chase plot in vain.

**Geocles the Mountainbreaker:** Long's Scion, you are the most important offering in this ritual sacrifice. No matter how strong your will is, it will eventually be swallowed by the memoria tide. When that time comes, you and the powers you bear will serve us...

**Geocles the Mountainbreaker:** We will use the Permanence to awaken the fallen Giant Dragon and give the "Earth" an everlasting body!

**Dan Heng:** I see. So you pretended to be Terravox...

**Dan Heng:** In order to lead us here into a trap.

**Geocles the Mountainbreaker:** If it weren't for the Imperator's tricks, the name "Terravox" would be shared between us anyway.

**"Hysilens":** They made the right choice for Amphoreus's tomorrow.

**Geocles the Mountainbreaker:** But the tomorrow you speak of only ever had a place for "humans."

**Geocles the Mountainbreaker:** Watch, my old friend...

**Geocles the Mountainbreaker:** I will once again start a fire that burns away the chains that shackle us!

**"Hysilens":** Why don't you leave this to me, Lord Dan Heng? Use that Hidden Passage to reach the opposite shore. Perhaps (Trailblazer) is there, deeper inside...

**Dan Heng:** ...

**Dan Heng:** No, I will stay here.

**"Dan Feng":** ...

**Dan Heng:** "Mountainbreaker," hero of the Mountain Dwellers. If I do not stay here and triumph over him, he will die an ugly death in delusions of the Permanence. That should not be his destiny.

**Dan Heng:** And for the vicious beast that insists on obstructing the Astral Express and threatening the fate of the world...

**Dan Heng:** We've stood on the precipice countless times along this journey, forced to make difficult choices in the face of disaster. But this time...

**Dan Heng:** It's my turn to put some pressure on this world.

*(Enter battle against Black Tide's Champion: Geocles the Mountainbreaker*1; Ichor Memosprite: Pheasant*2)*

**"Dan Feng":** This feeling, this scene... Hah, all too similar.

---

*(After the battle)*

**"Hysilens":** It's time to stop struggling, Geocles. Go to your death with dignity.

**Dan Heng:** Before that, tell me, what kind of deal did you make with Evernight? How was the great Mountainbreaker reduced to this?

**Geocles the Mountainbreaker:** ...

**???: ** *bereaved roar*

**"Dan Feng":** That's...

**Terravox:** Leave it to me. I'll recount the Earth's heaviest past...

**Terravox:** ...And sever its deepest delusion.

**"Hysilens":** Terravox... Is that you?

**"Hysilens":** Your body... has become so frail.

**Terravox:** Even mountain ranges grow old and eventually die.

**"Dan Feng":** But I can hear the sound of blood still surging through your veins, fast and strong...

**Dan Heng:** I suppose you are the true Earth demigod.

**Terravox:** I am near my end, and the demigod title means nothing to me now. The being who stands here now is just the former comrade of the "Mountainbreaker"...

**Terravox:** And the traitor who abandoned him.

**"Dan Feng":** Traitor?

**"Hysilens":** Terravox was once a member of Geocles' Dromas Legion. This force was famous for its skilled riders and their brave dromases.

**"Hysilens":** Terravox themself was the Dromas King, the Giant Dragon who swore their life to the "Mountainbreaker."

**"Hysilens":** In the battle against Georios, the Imperator could not surmount this mountain and thus had to break it from the inside. Terravox's betrayal was key to the Flame-Chase Army's victory.

**Terravox:** The age of the gods was coming to an end. Living beings had adapted to the changes amidst the rifts of the earth, and now they had to learn to survive in the crevices of the epics.

**Terravox:** Someone had to watch over the "Earth." So I agreed to a deal. I would take the Titan's divine authority and sink into the cliffs, using my flesh and blood forged of stone to mend the land. But...

**Terravox:** There was one "betrayal" I could never forget.

**Terravox:** The "Mountainbreaker"... refused to believe that was my choice, even till his dying breath. Faced with having to surrender to the Imperator, he chose to die instead.

**"Dan Feng":** So this is why you decided to conspire with "Evernight."

**Terravox:** "Time's" strange visitor appeared before me. The great power from beyond the sky called the Remembrance... It's incredible.

**Terravox:** Geocles was able to be reborn as a "memosprite." He mumbled about the times we fought side by side, just like how all the lives this body carries speak to me.

**Terravox:** "Evernight" did not ask for much in return. She only wished for me to use the divine authority of "Earth" to hide her tracks.

**"Hysilens":** Since you're being so candid, does this mean... you've given up resisting?

**Terravox:** Hah, resistance...

**Terravox:** For a beast on the brink of death, what is the point of resistance?

**"Hysilens":** I see... A thousand years have passed, and your mind has long since been shattered.

**Terravox:** This frail body bears the weight of all those I've betrayed, and now, my only wish is to rest with them...

**Terravox:** In the unchanging past.

**Dan Heng:** ...

**Terravox:** Go, Long's Scion. That fiery blaze has already burned through "Earth's" protection, revealing the secrets underneath...

**Terravox:** The deepest depths in Amphoreus. The great tomb where all the past is buried.

**Terravox:** The one you seek, "Evernight," walked out of that place. Before her arrival, no one knew of its existence.

**"Dan Feng":** In other words, that is the place where "Evernight" came into being...

**"Hysilens":** And it is also where she is now.

**Dan Heng:** ...

**Dan Heng:** You sank willingly into the past, and now your "memories" have turned into this form... Was it worth it?

**Terravox:** At least I still remember.

**Terravox:** As long as I never forget, they will live on.

**Dan Heng:** Primitive and animalistic... I understand these instincts.

**Dan Heng:** Have you ever regretted anything?

**Terravox:** The earth is not a lonely island in the middle of the ocean. It is the sum of all the living things that stand on it.

**Terravox:** I have never regretted defending the creatures of this world.

**Dan Heng:** Then, a final question...

---

**Dan Heng:** Terravox... After all this, how much are you willing to sacrifice for the power of the Permanence?

**Terravox:** ...

**Terravox:** Everything.

**Terravox:** My journey up to here has allowed me to fully understood my destiny: No matter how I struggle, the "Earth" will never be able to reach the stars.

**Terravox:** But my compatriots deserve to continue their struggle. At the end of my life, I want to make one last effort to give them a chance to move on to the future...

**Terravox:** Even if it is a future I am not able to witness.

**"Dan Feng":** ...

**Dan Heng:** I understand. Then...

**Dan Heng:** Join me in rebelling against this unjust fate once more.

**Dan Heng:** Even if the world collapses, the "Earth" must remain unshaken.

**Dan Heng:** Give me the Coreflame that burns within you, and allow this immortal body of the Permanence to bear the furnace of life for you.

**Terravox:** Long's Scion... Are you willing to bring them all into the new world?

**Dan Heng:** I won't give you empty promises. Allow me to answer your question from a different perspective.

**Dan Heng:** The Permanence is the path that dragons trod, and "eternity" is life's deepest desire. If you entrust the earth's living beings to me, they will live in symbiosis with the instincts in my blood.

**Dan Heng:** With this body as an ark, I will deliver them to tomorrow for you. That tomorrow won't be the end of Amphoreus's west wind...

**Dan Heng:** But a vast and boundless sea of stars.

**Terravox:** A sea of stars...

**Dan Heng:** It's a future where all beings can Trailblaze side by side, not a prison made from Remembrance.

**Terravox:** In the Veil of Evernight's memories, you've always... been someone with reservations.

**Terravox:** Hah, I misjudged your resolve. I understand... This is the only choice.

**Terravox:** Behold, this Coreflame that burns in my chest. This Coreflame born from the "Earth." It is the redsoil from which life sprouts, and the furnace of all living things...

**Terravox:** It tells me to commit one final act of treason: Entrust this heavy task to the Trailblazers from beyond the sky. Let them tend to the wild terrain and safeguard the lands...

**Terravox:** And thus bring the endless stream of life into the distant future.

**Dan Heng:** I promise you, Terravox.

**Terravox:** Good. When I hand over the divine authority, this place will fall to pieces.

**Terravox:** Are you prepared to hold up the Sacred Tree if it falls?

**Dan Heng:** Of course.

---

**Terravox:** Then free what remains of my body and let me give you this flame that turned away from the Flame-Chase—

**Terravox:** I now relinquish my duty as a demigod.

**Terravox:** "You shall dig your own grave and burn in the furnace of rebellion"...

**Terravox:** I only hope that, after all this time, Father will take me back into their heart.

**Dan Heng:** Come, crouching beast! For the endless life of this world...

**Dan Heng:** Rise from your dormancy, and follow me as soaring dragons of the wilderness!

---

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Terravox:** Take it...

**Terravox:** My final rebellion...

**Terravox:** ...Then, declare to the "Earth" the name of the new god!

**Dan Heng:** I am "Dan Heng"...

**Dan Heng:** The one who guards the Trailblaze's way forward!

*(Cutscene ends)*

---

**"Dan Feng":** Fortunately...

**"Dan Feng":** The worst did not come to pass.

**"Dan Feng":** If your opponent had already turned into a vicious beast, whatever you did wouldn't weigh on your conscience... So what if that demigod had harbored ill intentions or vowed to fight to the death...

**"Dan Feng":** Would you have pointed your spear at them and carved out the Coreflame with your own hands?

**Dan Heng:** There's no point in discussing "what-ifs," though I am glad it didn't come to that.

**Dan Heng:** Regardless, I will attempt to join this game and become a part of the Era Nova.

**Dan Heng:** This is the only way I can face my friends and this world with a "clear conscience."

**"Dan Feng":** Hah...

**"Dan Feng":** Now that things are settled between us, it's time for you and I to say goodbye.

**"Dan Feng":** Everything that I left behind has faded away at this point. Go and march toward your own "Permanence."

**"Dan Feng":** As for how to tame it... I'll leave that to you.

**Dan Heng:** Thank you. We can't go back to the past and make better choices. But at least we can do better in the future.

**Dan Heng:** A dragon can only welcome new life by shedding away his old, scarred skin...

**Dan Heng:** For the first time in my life, I feel at ease.

**"Dan Feng":** This burning Coreflame... Is this what the Destruction feels like?

**"Dan Feng":** The light is unexpectedly warm... It's just like...

**"Dan Feng":** The fleeting moments... when one wakes from a dream...

**Dan Heng:** Farewell, Dan Feng...

**Dan Heng:** Now... it's time for me to set out once more.

---

**Dan Heng:** Is even more unshakeable than the "Earth" itself.

### Part 153 — `databank/missions/chapter-07-back-to-earth.md`
**Context:** Chapter 7 — Back to Earth in Evernight (v3.6) > Mission 7 — Great Tomb, Hide the Secrets of Incarnations > Talk with Dan Heng
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-07-back-to-earth__talk-with-dan-heng`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-07-back-to-earth.md`
**Witness:** spoke

*(Dan Heng, your most trusted Express Guard, and the most reliable companion on your Trailblaze journey. He accepted Earth's divine authority, solely to stand alongside you and the Chrysos Heir friends, facing the new world and the impending final clash together. Just knowing he's back in Amphoreus gives you not just one, but a hundred times more peace of mind. When his consciousness delves into the memoria tide where no one else can reach, he is the bedrock, the waves, and the frosty gales... He is the Earth and all that exists upon it. He came for you, and now you will both go for March. The blazing sun has shown the way. Press onward.)*

**Dan Heng:** Finally...

**Dan Heng:** I've found you, (Trailblazer).

> *(Trailblazer)* Dan Heng, it's been a while...
> **(Trailblazer):** It really has... been a long time.
> **Dan Heng:** Don't be so sentimental. That's not like you.
> *(Trailblazer)* Dan Heng, did you... get taller?
> **(Trailblazer):** Long time no see. Did you... get taller?
> **Dan Heng:** Heh. While you were trapped, I experienced... a lot.
> *(Trailblazer)* I've been waiting for you for so long, Dan Heng.
> **(Trailblazer):** Dan Heng, I knew... you would come back.
> **Dan Heng:** Heh. While you were trapped, I experienced... a lot.

---

**Dan Heng:** Thankfully, "Evernight" did not try to stop me. Looks like we've managed to stay ahead of her this time.

> *(Trailblazer)* We must save March 7th.
> **Dan Heng:** Rest assured, we won't abandon her. We'll all return to the Express together... No one will be left behind.
> *(Trailblazer)* What about Cyrene? Where is she?
> **Dan Heng:** You mean "Mem"? Sorry, I haven't seen her at all on this journey. Perhaps you were not the only one being held captive by "Evernight."
> *(Trailblazer)* How's the Express Crew doing?
> **Dan Heng:** They never once stopped worrying about us, about you, me, or March 7th. They never gave up on trying to save us, even from outside this world.
> **Dan Heng:** Miss Himeko, Mr. Yang, Pom-Pom, and even Sunday, Black Swan, and the two geniuses... They are all doing everything in their power to aid us.
> *(Trailblazer)* She was everywhere in the memoria tide...
> **Dan Heng:** I understand. Her power... shouldn't be underestimated.

**Dan Heng:** It was only when I personally stepped into the maze of "Time" that it suddenly struck me... She and I had crossed paths once before.

**Dan Heng:** I'll only warn you once. Leave her body. Now.

**Dan Heng:** I feel like I've seen these floating black things somewhere before...

**Dan Heng:** I had an unexpected encounter with her when I left Amphoreus. Perhaps to avoid exposing her whereabouts, she stripped that memory from my mind.

**Dan Heng:** That power called "Oblivion"... it's just like an Emanator's.

**(Trailblazer):** (Oblivion...)

> *(Trailblazer)* She wants to erase "Worldbearing's" memories.
> *(Trailblazer)* She planned to make me forget everything...
> **Dan Heng:** I know. But you still remember me and our companions for now.

> *(Trailblazer)* Nothing seems out of the ordinary...
> **Dan Heng:** If I'm not mistaken, Evernight's plan is to make you forget Amphoreus, including the Chrysos Heirs who have journeyed with us up till now.
> *(Trailblazer)* "Mere mortals. Flawed individuals. Failed heroes..."
> **Dan Heng:** Are those "Evernight's" exact words? If I'm not mistaken, she intends to make you forget Amphoreus, including the Chrysos Heirs who have journeyed with us up till now.

**Dan Heng:** But it seems you still recognize Lady Hysilens.

**"Hysilens":** If I made that much of an impression on Little Gray Fry, then I am naturally delighted.

**"Hysilens":** But I fear your escape from that power they call "Oblivion"...

**"Hysilens":** Is thanks to someone else.

> *(Trailblazer)* Castorice...?
> *(Trailblazer)* Was it you... who protected me?

**"Castorice":** Nice to meet you, or... maybe I should ask, how have you been since we last saw each other?

**"Hysilens":** Along with you, this little butterflyfish's memory was also salvaged from the depths of that whirlpool.

**"Hysilens":** Just as a pearl is cradled within its shell... the power of Thanatos surrounds you, shielding your soul from corrosion.

**"Castorice":** Miss Cyrene has spoken of you often to me, (Trailblazer). Though we never met in this life, I believe... there's a kind of warmth that can transcend time itself.

**"Castorice":** Through the endless flow of time, I guarded the secret passage between Styxia and the Vortex. When the memoria tide flowed backwards into the River of Souls, even in deep slumber, I could feel that warmth...

**"Castorice":** And with all my might, I protected that warmth.

**"Castorice":** I apologize if that seemed improper. I hope you'll forgive me.

> *(Trailblazer)* Thank you, Castorice.
> *(Trailblazer)* In the end, you were there for me.
> *(Trailblazer)* See, I really am a heartwarming guy deep down.
> **Dan Heng:** She was just speaking metaphorically... This conversation feels familiar somehow.

**"Castorice":** Haha...

**"Castorice":** I understand that your mission is still unfinished. The path ahead is treacherous, and even though my body is bound to these dark currents, I'll do everything I can to watch over you both.

**"Hysilens":** The little butterflyfish is right. There is still a pair of eyes watching from the shadows, but we won't let them get anywhere near you.

**"Hysilens":** Go forth, golden dragonfish, and you, "Deliverer," seek out your companion...

**"Hysilens":** Go forth and illuminate the lightless "Evernight."

**Dan Heng:** I cannot thank you enough. Accept this promise from the Nameless: Amphoreus will definitely write a brand-new ending for itself.

**"Castorice":** Of course, we'll write it together.

> *(Trailblazer)* Thank you, Hysilens.
> *(Trailblazer)* Thank you too, Castorice.
> **(Trailblazer):** The Nameless will never stop...

**Dan Heng:** Until all of us stand side by side in the true new world.

**Cipher:** Look. I dug three thousand feet down, and finally struck gold. *(A golden feather)* Look familiar? It's from that little Deliverer boy. This ruin is the "seal" he built from the shattered remains of thirty million lifetimes.

**Phainon (echo):** There is no need to question your heart, no need to crave victory. If I am born to be a vessel of sin... Then I shall roar at evil, and Trailblaze a dawn for future generations to come!

**Dan Heng:** I see... So this is how he trapped Irontomb to buy time for the world outside.

**Cipher:** Sounds just like him. People say cats have nine lives, but dying and coming back, millions and millions of times... no one walks away from that in one piece. He's probably the only one who could've endured that.

> *(Trailblazer)* Phainon's not facing this alone anymore.
> *(Trailblazer)* He believed we would come...

**Cipher:** Hey, Deliverer boy! Sorry for before, but the heroes you were always holding out for... they're finally here now! So hang in there a bit longer! Sorry to keep you waiting all this time!

**Dan Heng:** Please join us, Cipher. Help us fight for a new sunrise tomorrow.
**Cyrene:** This time, its light will stand without lies.

**Cipher:** Well, would you look at that. I ended up being one of the heroes after all, huh? Alright, then write it like this: Cipher, Trickery's Traveler... All she ever wanted was for there always to be water in deserts and gold in fertile soil, and for even a thief to steal their way into legend! "Here, take the Coin of Whimsy. When it's time to make a quick getaway, just toss it high into the sky."

*(Obtain: Trickery Coin)*

---

**Castorice, Hand of Shadow:** So even this vessel... you've forged into a shackle? I'm glad to see you again... (Trailblazer). Looking back, I had only one act of "salvation" to my name, and only one life I ever saved. To bring hope to a world so vast with these hands... Even in dreams, it always felt like too much to ask for.

> *(Trailblazer)* We are saving countless lives.
> *(Trailblazer)* We're bound for the vast, endless sky.

**Castorice:** Mm. I... won't forget.

*(Flame Reaver appears)*

**Dan Heng:** Flame Reaver? Why is he here?

**Castorice:** This is the first Khaslana, and Lord Phainon's deepest wound. He remembered everything he bore through the fires of his fury, but when the embers faded, all that remained was cold regret. I've restrained countless vessels like this across endless cycles. But this one... the pain he carried runs deeper than any I've known.

> **Dan Heng:** ...I can already tell I'll have nightmares tonight.
> **Apophis:** Goodness! The Ghost Seal's obsession has grown even stronger!
> **Dan Heng:** You needn't concern yourself over me. You've got the final against Bubbles coming up. Are you ready to continue?
> *(Trailblazer)* Absolutely, strike while the iron's hot!
> *(Trailblazer)* I need a break first.

### Part 196 — `databank/missions/adventure/v35-37.md`
**Context:** Adventure Missions — Versions 3.5–3.7 > As I've Written > Dialogue > Return to the Express and bring this journey to a close
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `v35-37__return-to-the-express-and-bring-this-journey-to-a-close`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/v35-37.md`
**Witness:** spoke

***(Trailblazer):*** Perhaps this is the final step of "Genesis."
**???: **What are you spacing out for?
> *(Trailblazer)* Oh, it's you guys...
> *(Trailblazer)* You finished your missions, too?
>
> **March 7th:** Of course! After all, we three trailblazed through Amphoreus together. I ran my heart out for this!
> **Dan Heng:** I received a message from March when I was leaving Thalassa, so I came back with her. She ran into some trouble on Kalsas.
> **March 7th:** Don't even get me started. I'm already shy as it is, and then the Synesthesia Beacon had to break down... Thank goodness Dan Heng can speak the Clayese language.
> **Dan Heng:** Also, I discovered a new species resembling chimeras on Spindle-III. There were no prior records of it.
> **March 7th:** Does that mean there could be planets completely filled with dromases?
> **Dan Heng:** Let's wait for Himeko and Mr. Yang to come back before we do a full debrief. You should rest for now, (Trailblazer), you look tired.
> **March 7th:** Yeah. See you in a bit then!
